They drive the REAL run entry (<see cref=\"RunCommand\"/>, which builds its scheduler through\n/// the production <see cref=\"Guardrails.Core.Execution.SchedulerFactory\"/>) over committed fixture\n/// plans that carry a plan-level <c>&lt;plan&gt;/preflights/</c> folder (understood by the\n/// four-folder loader, Deliverable 6), and assert OBSERVABLE outcomes: the process exit code and the\n/// journal's new top-level <c>planPreflights</c> section in <c>state/run.json</c> (Deliverable 4).\n///\n/// <para>\n/// Every test runs in <b>BOTH</b> serial (<c>maxParallelism = 1</c>) and worktree\n/// (<c>maxParallelism &gt; 1</c>, a git workspace) mode via a two-row <see cref=\"TheoryAttribute\"/>.\n/// The serial-mode path is exactly where a false-green hides: the pre-DAG phase reuses the\n/// <c>IReVerifier</c> seam that the composition root now wires UNCONDITIONALLY (Deliverable 1), so a\n/// regression that only fired the phase in worktree mode would pass a worktree-only test and ship a\n/// broken serial run.\n/// </para>\n///\n/// <para>\n/// These are RED on current code — there is no pre-DAG phase yet, so a red\n/// <c>&lt;plan&gt;/preflights/</c> does NOT halt, and the <c>planPreflights</c> journal section is\n/// never written (the loader parses the folder but nothing evaluates it). Each test therefore fails\n/// today (the intent) and goes green once Deliverable 3 lands the phase + its SSOT §7 resume rule.\n/// Nothing here IMPLEMENTS the phase; the fixtures are the only new surface, and they live entirely\n/// in this one file (the task's write-scope).\n/// </para>\n///\n/// <para>\n/// Tagged <c>[Trait(\"Category\", \"Preflights\")]</c> at the class level (inherited by every case) so the\n/// per-deliverable green baseline can exclude these deliberately-red tests via\n/// <c>--filter \"Category!=Preflights\"</c>.\n/// </para>\n/// </summary>\n[Trait(\"Category\", \"Preflights\")]\npublic sealed class PlanPreflightPhaseTests\n{\n    // Serial fixtures use maxParallelism 1; worktree fixtures use maxParallelism 2 (the git workspace\n    // + parallelism combination the factory wires a GitWorktreeProvider on). On current code (no pre-DAG phase) the loader\n    // parses the red preflight but never evaluates it, so the green DAG runs to exit 0 → the exit\n    // assertion (and the marker/zero-attempts assertions) FAIL. That RED bar is the point.\n    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    [Theory]\n    [InlineData(1)] // serial mode\n    [InlineData(2)] // worktree mode\n    public async Task PlanPreflightRed_HaltsBeforeAnyTask_ZeroAttempts_Exit2(int maxParallelism)\n    {\n        using var repo = new TempGitRepo();\n        string planDir = CreatePlan(repo.RepoPath, maxParallelism);\n        WritePlanPreflight(planDir, PreflightKind.Red);\n        WriteTask(planDir, \"01-only\", createBaselineArtifact: false, guardrailPasses: true);\n\n        int exit = await RunCliAsync(planDir);\n\n        // The red pre-DAG phase must halt the run at exit 2 (ExitCodes.TaskFailed) — NOT the exit 0 a\n        // wholly-green DAG produces on current code, which never evaluates the preflight.\n        Assert.Equal(ExitCodes.TaskFailed, exit);\n\n        JournalDocument journal = ReadJournal(planDir);\n\n        // The F9 plan-scoped halt is journaled OUTSIDE tasks{} as planPreflights.status = plan-preflight-failed.\n        Assert.NotNull(journal.PlanPreflights);\n        Assert.Equal(PlanPhaseStatus.PlanPreflightFailed, journal.PlanPreflights!.Status);\n\n        // Zero-token halt: the run stopped BEFORE scheduling, so not one task attempt was journaled.\n        Assert.NotEmpty(journal.Tasks);\n        Assert.All(journal.Tasks.Values, entry => Assert.Empty(entry.Attempts));\n    }\n\n    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    // Behavior #2 — a GREEN <plan>/preflights/ records planPreflights.status == passed with a\n    // planHash matching the current PlanHash, and the DAG then schedules normally. On current code\n    // the DAG also runs to exit 0, but the planPreflights section is never written → the NotNull\n    // assertion is the RED bar.\n    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    [Theory]\n    [InlineData(1)] // serial mode\n    [InlineData(2)] // worktree mode\n    public async Task PlanPreflightGreen_MarkerPassed_WithMatchingPlanHash_DagSchedules(int maxParallelism)\n    {\n        using var repo = new TempGitRepo();\n        string planDir = CreatePlan(repo.RepoPath, maxParallelism);\n        WritePlanPreflight(planDir, PreflightKind.Green);\n        WriteTask(planDir, \"01-only\", createBaselineArtifact: false, guardrailPasses: true);\n\n        int exit = await RunCliAsync(planDir);\n\n        // A green pre-DAG lets the DAG schedule to a wholly-green exit 0. Assert.Equal(JournalTaskStatus.Succeeded, journal2.Tasks[\"02-consumer\"].Status);\n    }\n\n    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n    // Fixture helpers — committed plan folders inside a temp git repo. Self-contained (the\n    // TempGitRepo pattern is copied here because the task's write-scope is this one file), mirroring\n    // the git-worktree fixtures in GitHookIsolationTests / StagingOutputsRunTests. Scripts are\n    // OS-appropriate (PowerShell on Windows, bash elsewhere) so the same test runs cross-platform.\n    // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n    private enum PreflightKind\n    {\n        /// <summary>A pre-DAG check that always passes (exit 0).</summary>\n        Green,\n\n        /// <summary>A pre-DAG check that always fails (exit 1) — the plan-preflight-red halt.</summary>\n        Red,\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// A B1 negative baseline: passes only while <c>baseline-artifact.txt</c> is ABSENT from the\n        /// workspace (fails once a task introduces it). Evaluated exactly once across the run lifecycle.\n        /// </summary>\n        NegativeBaseline\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Create the plan folder at <c>&lt;repo&gt;/plan/</c> with <c>workspace: \"..\"</c> (so the git repo\n    /// root is the workspace — worktree mode's hard dependency) and <paramref name=\"maxParallelism\"/>.\n    /// <c>defaultRetries: 0</c> keeps single-attempt semantics exact.\n    /// </summary>\n    private static string CreatePlan(string repoPath, int maxParallelism)\n    {\n        string planDir = Path.Combine(repoPath, \"plan\");\n        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(planDir, \"tasks\"));\n\n        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(planDir, \"guardrails.json\"),\n            $$\"\"\"\n            {\n              \"version\": 1,\n              \"guardrailMode\": \"failFast\",\n              \"workspace\": \"..\",\n              \"defaultRetries\": 0,\n              \"maxParallelism\": {{maxParallelism}}\n            }\n            \"\"\");\n\n        return planDir;\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Write a plan-level <c>&lt;plan&gt;/preflights/</c> \"Full Flight Check\". The file is\n    /// guardrail-shaped and opens with a <c>catches:</c> comment (the four-folder loader requires it),\n    /// followed by the deterministic check the <paramref name=\"kind\"/> selects.\n    /// </summary>\n    private static void WritePlanPreflight(string planDir, PreflightKind kind)\n    {\n        string dir = Path.Combine(planDir, \"preflights\");\n        Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);\n\n        (string ps, string bash) = kind switch\n        {\n            PreflightKind.Green => (\n                \"exit 0\",\n                \"exit 0\"),\n            PreflightKind.Red => (\n                \"Write-Output 'plan preflight red (deliberate)'\\nexit 1\",\n                \"echo 'plan preflight red (deliberate)'\\nexit 1\"),\n            PreflightKind.NegativeBaseline => (\n                \"if (Test-Path (Join-Path $env:GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE 'baseline-artifact.txt')) { Write-Output 'baseline artifact already present'; exit 1 } else { exit 0 }\",\n                \"if [ -f \\\"$GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/baseline-artifact.txt\\\" ]; then echo 'baseline artifact already present'; exit 1; else exit 0; fi\"),\n            _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(kind))\n        };\n\n        const string catches = \"# catches: 01-baseline - a pre-DAG phase that never evaluates the plan preflights against the starting bytes\";\n        WriteScript(Path.Combine(dir, PreflightFile), catches + \"\\n\" + ps, catches + \"\\n\" + bash);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Write a task: <c>task.json</c> (with any Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(taskDir, \"guardrails\"));\n\n        string deps = dependsOn.Length == 0\n            ? \"[]\"\n            : \"[\" + string.Join(\", \", dependsOn.Select(d => {{id}}\",\n              \"dependsOn\": {{deps}}\n            }\n            \"\"\");\n\n        string ps = $\"New-Item -Path (Join-Path $env:GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE '{id}.out') -Force -Value 'ran' |      int revalExit = await RunCliAsync(\"run\", plan.PlanDir, \"--revalidate-task\", \"plan:guardrails\");\n276\t        Assert.Equal(ExitCodes.Success, revalExit);\n277\t    }\n278\t\n279\t    /// <summary>\n280\t    /// Worktree mode B2 revalidate, still-failing gate: with NO fix, <c>--revalidate-task plan:guardrails</c>\n281\t    /// re-runs the RED <c>&lt;plan&gt;/guardrails/</c> checks A forced-fail spy\n306\t    /// rolls exactly that non-FF sibling back to <c>needs-human</c> — an outcome only the DURING-RUN per-union\n307\t    /// re-verify can produce (the end-of-run terminal gate fires only when the whole run is green). The spy\n308\t    /// also captures that the re-verify carried the integration set, proving it was not absorbed.\n309\t    /// GREEN on both current and new code by design.\n310\t    /// </summary>\n311\t    [Fact]\n312\t    public async Task PerUnionIntegrationReVerify_StillFiresAtUnions_NotAbsorbedByTerminalFolder()\n313\t    {\n314\t        using var repo = new SiblingUnionRepo();\n315\t\n316\t        var spy = new SpyReVerifier { AlwaysPass = false };\n317\t        var provider = new GitWorktreeProvider(repo.RepoPath, repo.WorktreeRoot);\n318\t\n319\t        RunReport report = await RunSchedulerAsync(repo.PlanDir, provider, spy);\n320\t\n321\t        // The per-union re-verify fired DURING the run...\n322\t        Assert.True(spy.CallCount > 0,\n323\t            \"the §4.3 per-union re-verify must still fire at a non-FF union — SpyReVerifier was never called\");\n324\t\n325\t        // ...carrying the scope:\"integration\" set (not an empty/absorbed set)...\n326\t        Assert.Contains(spy.CallGuardrails, set => set.Any(g =>\n327\t            string.Equals(g.Scope, \"integration\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)));\n328\t\n329\t        // ...and forced-failed, rolling exactly the non-FF sibling back to needs-human (one sibling FF's green).\n330\t        Assert.Single(report.Tasks, t => t.Outcome == TaskOutcome.NeedsHuman);\n331\t        Assert.Single(report.Tasks, t => t.Outcome == TaskOutcome.Succeeded);\n332\t    }\n333\t\n334\t    // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n335\t    // Behaviour 5 (issue #240) — the terminal phase's success path is bracketed with plain console\n336\t    // lines, matching Full Flight Checks' treatment (PlanPreflightPhaseTests). }\n353\t\n354\t    [Fact]\n355\t    public async Task NoPlanGuardrails_PrintsNothingAboutTerminalGate()\n356\t    {\n357\t        // A plan with no <plan>/guardrails/ folder at all opts out of this Assert.DoesNotContain(\"Terminal Gate: passed.\", output);\n382\t    }\n383\t\n384\t    /// <summary>\n385\t    /// Hand-wire the REAL <see cref=\"Scheduler\"/> over the given worktree provider + Load-only (no validate) so the fan-out\n387\t    /// plan need not carry a terminal folder for this per-union pin.\n388\t    /// </summary>\n389\t    private static async Task<RunReport> RunSchedulerAsync(\n390\t        string planDir, IWorktreeProvider provider, IReVerifier reVerifier)\n391\t    {\n392\t        PlanLoadResult load = new PlanLoader().Load(planDir);\n393\t        Assert.NotNull(load.Plan);\n394\t        Assert.False(load.HasErrors, string.Join(\"\\n\", load.Diagnostics));\n395\t\n396\t        var stateManager = new StateManager(load.Plan!.PlanDirectory);\n397\t        stateManager.Initialize();\n398\t\n399\t        RunJournal journal = RunJournal.LoadOrCreate(load.Plan!);\n400\t\n401\t        var registry = PromptRunnerRegistry.Build(load.Plan!.Config,\n402\t            _ => throw new InvalidOperationException(\"No prompt runners in the plan-guardrail per-union test.\"));\n403\t        var interpreterMap = new InterpreterMap(new PathExecutableProbe(), load.Plan!.Config.Interpreters);\n404\t\n405\t        var executor = new TaskExecutor(\n406\t            load.Plan!, new ProcessRunner(), interpreterMap, stateManager, journal, IRunObserver.Null, registry);\n407\t\n408\t        var scheduler = new Scheduler(\n409\t            load.Plan!, executor, journal, worktreeProvider: provider, reVerifier: reVerifier);\n410\t\n411\t        return await scheduler.RunAsync(load.Plan!, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);\n412\t    }\n413\t\n414\t    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n415\t    // SpyReVerifier — records every re-verify call and the guardrail set it carried.\n416\t    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n417\t\n418\t    private sealed class SpyReVerifier : IReVerifier\n419\t    {\n420\t        public int CallCount { get; private set; }\n421\t        public bool AlwaysPass { get; init; } = true;\n422\t        public List<IReadOnlyList<GuardrailDefinition>> CallGuardrails { get; } = [];\n423\t\n424\t        public Task<ReVerifyResult> ReVerifyAsync(\n425\t            string worktreePath,\n426\t            IReadOnlyList<GuardrailDefinition> guardrails,\n427\t            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n428\t        {\n429\t            CallCount++;\n430\t            CallGuardrails.Add(guardrails);\n431\t            return Task.FromResult(new ReVerifyResult { Passed = AlwaysPass });\n432\t        }\n433\t    }\n434\t\n435\t    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n436\t    // PlanGuardrailPlan — a linear-chain plan (01-a → 02-b, both green) plus a plan-level\n437\t    // <plan>/guardrails/ terminal gate. File.ReadAllLines(ActionRanLogPath).Count(l => l.Trim().Length > 0)\n460\t            : 0;\n461\t\n462\t        public PlanGuardrailPlan(bool worktree)\n463\t        {\n464\t            _root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), \"gr-plangr-\" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString(\"N\"));\n465\t            Directory.CreateDirectory(_root);\n466\t            ActionRanLogPath = Path.Combine(_root, \"action-ran.log\");\n467\t\n468\t            if (worktree)\n469\t            {\n470\t                RepoPath = Path.Combine(_root, \"repo\");\n471\t                Directory.CreateDirectory(RepoPath);\n472\t                InitRepo(RepoPath);\n473\t                PlanDir = Path.Combine(RepoPath, \"plan\");\n474\t            }\n475\t            else\n476\t            {\n477\t                PlanDir = Path.Combine(_root, \"plan\");\n478\t            }\n479\t\n480\t            Directory.CreateDirectory(PlanDir);\n481\t            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(PlanDir, \"state\"));\n482\t            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(PlanDir, \"tasks\"));\n483\t\n484\t            int maxParallelism = worktree ? 2 : 1;\n485\t            string workspace = worktree ? \"..\" : \".\";\n486\t            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(PlanDir, \"guardrails.json\"),\n487\t                \"{\\n\" +\n488\t                \"  \\\"version\\\": 1,\\n\" +\n489\t                \"  \\\"guardrailMode\\\": \\\"failFast\\\",\\n\" +\n490\t                \"  \\\"workspace\\\": \\\"\" + workspace + \"\\\",\\n\" +\n491\t                \"  \\\"defaultRetries\\\": 0,\\n\" +\n492\t                \"  \\\"maxParallelism\\\": \" + maxParallelism + \"\\n\" +\n493\t                \"}\\n\");\n494\t\n495\t            // Linear chain: one leaf, no fan-in ⇒ the worktree content-teeth rule (GR2028) is exempt, so a\n496\t            // plain exit-1 terminal check validates clean. No task carries scope:\"integration\", so today's\n497\t            // integrationGate terminal gate stays dormant and the run drains green (exit 0) on current code.\n498\t            WriteTask(\"01-a\");\n499\t            WriteTask(\"02-b\", \"01-a\");\n500\t\n501\t            string terminalDir = Path.Combine(PlanDir, \"guardrails\");\n502\t TerminalCheckScript(passes: false));\n505\t        }\n506\t\n507\t        /// <summary>Flip the terminal gate green (the human's hand-fix to the merged HEAD).</summary>\n508\t        public void HandFixTerminalGate() => WriteScript(TerminalCheckPath, TerminalCheckScript(passes: true));\n509\t\n510\t        /// <summary>Run git in the (worktree-mode) repo and return stdout.</summary>\n511\t        public string Git(params string[] args) => RunGit(RepoPath!, args);\n512\t\n513\t        private void WriteTask(string id, params string[] dependsOn)\n514\t        {\n515\t            string           string deps = dependsOn.Length == 0\n520\t                ? \"[]\"\n521\t                : \"[\" + string.Join(\", \", dependsOn.Select(d => \"\\\"\" + d + \"\\\"\")) + \"]\";\n522\t            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(taskDir, \"task.json\"),\n523\t                \"{ \\\"description\\\": \\\"plan-guardrail task \" + id + \"\\\", \\\"dependsOn\\\": \" + deps + \"           {\n583\t                // A scope:\"integration\" task guardrail — the §4.3 per-union set the re-verify must re-run.\n584\t                WriteScript(Path.Combine(grDir, Ps ? }\");\n586\t            }\n587\t        }\n588\t\n589\t        public void Dispose() => SafeDeleteTree(_root);\n590\t    }\n591\t\n592\t    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n593\t    // Shared script + git + teardown helpers. Paths use '/' inside the workspace (PowerShell accepts\n594\t    // it) so nothing needs backslash-escaping; absolute log paths are inserted at runtime (never as a\n595\t    // string literal), so they carry OS-native separators harmlessly.\n596\t    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n597\t\n598\t    /// <summary>An action that writes <c>src/&lt;id&gt;.cs</c> into the workspace AND appends its id to an absolute log.</summary>\n599\t    private static string ActionScriptWithLog(string id, string logPath)\n600\t    {\n601\t        string safe = id.Replace(\"-\", \"_\");\n602\t        if (Ps)\n603\t        {\n604\t            return\n605\t                \"New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path \\\"$env:GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/src\\\" | Out-Null\\n\" +\n606\t                \"Set-Content -NoNewline -Path \\\"$env:GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/src/\" + id + \".cs\\\" -Value 'class \" + safe + \" {}'\\n\" +\n607\t                \"Add-Content -Path '\" + logPath + \"' -Value '\" + id + \"'\\n\" +\n608\t                \"exit 0\\n\";\n609\t        }\n610\t        return\n611\t            \"#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n\" +\n612\t            \"mkdir -p \\\"$GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/src\\\"\\n\" +\n613\t            \"printf 'class \" + safe + \" {}' > \\\"$GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/src/\" + id + \".cs\\\"\\n\" +\n614\t            \"printf '%s\\\\n' '\" + id + \"' >> '\" + logPath + \"'\\n\" +\n615\t            \"exit 0\\n\";\n616\t    }\n617\t\n618\t    /// <summary>An action that just writes <c>src/&lt;id&gt;.cs</c> into the workspace (so the segment commit is non-empty).</summary>\n619\t    private static string SimpleActionScript(string id)\n620\t    {\n621\t        string safe = id.Replace(\"-\", \"_\");\n622\t        if (Ps)\n623\t        {\n624\t            return\n625\t                \"New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path \\\"$env:GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/src\\\" | Out-Null\\n\" +\n626\t                \"Set-Content -NoNewline -Path \\\"$env:GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/src/\" + id + \".cs\\\" -Value 'class \" + safe + \" {}'\\n\" +\n627\t                \"exit 0\\n\";\n628\t        }\n629\t        return\n630\t            \"#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n\" +\n631\t            \"mkdir -p \\\"$GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/src\\\"\\n\" +\n632\t            \"printf 'class \" + safe + \" {}' > \\\"$GUARDRAILS_WORKSPACE/src/\" + id + \".cs\\\"\\n\" +\n633\t            \"exit 0\\n\";\n634\t    }\n635\t\n636\t    private static string GreenGuardrailScript() => Ps ? \"exit 0\\n\" : \"#!/usr/bin/env bash\\nexit 0\\n\";\n637\t\n638\t    /// <summary>\n639\t    /// The plan-level <c>&lt;plan&gt;/guardrails/</c> terminal check. Opens with the required <c>catches:</c>\n640\t    /// declaration (GR2027, enforced on plan-level folders). RED (exit 1) until hand-fixed to green (exit 0).\n641\t    /// </summary>\n642\t    private static string TerminalCheckScript(bool passes)\n643\t    {\n644\t        int code = passes ? 0 : 1;\n645\t        string verdict = passes ? \"green (hand-fixed)\" : \"RED (deliberate)\";\n646\t        if (Ps)\n647\t        {\n648\t            return\n649\t                \"# catches: the plan-level <plan>/guardrails/ terminal gate must fire on the merged HEAD and\\n\" +\n650\t                \"#          halt the run when a check exits non-zero; a terminal phase that never evaluates\\n\" +\n651\t                \"#          <plan>/guardrails/ leaves this file's verdict undetected (the wrong impl this catches).\\n\" +\n652\t                \"Write-Output 'plan-guardrail terminal gate \" + verdict + \"'\\n\" +\n653\t                \"exit \" + code + \"\\n\";\n654\t        }\n655\t        return\n656\t            \"#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n\" +\n657\t            \"# catches: the plan-level <plan>/guardrails/ terminal gate must fire on the merged HEAD and halt\\n\" +\n658\t            \"#          the run when a check exits non-zero.\\n\" +\n659\t            \"echo 'plan-guardrail terminal gate \" + verdict + \"'\\n\" +\n660\t            \"exit \" + code + \"\\n\";\n661\t    }\n662\t\n663\t    private static void WriteScript(string path, string content)\n664\t    {\n665\t        File.WriteAllText(path, content);\n666\t        if (!OperatingSystem.IsWindows())\n667\t        {\n668\t            File.SetUnixFileMode(path,\n669\t                UnixFileMode.UserRead | UnixFileMode.UserWrite | UnixFileMode.UserExecute |\n670\t                UnixFileMode.GroupRead | UnixFileMode.OtherRead);\n671\t        }\n672\t    }\n673\t\n674\t    private static void InitRepo(string repoPath)\n675\t    {\n676\t        RunGit(repoPath, \"init\");\n677\t        RunGit(repoPath, \"config\", \"user.email\", \"test@guardrails.local\");\n678\t        RunGit(repoPath, \"config\", \"user.name\", \"Guardrails Test\");\n679\t        RunGit(repoPath, \"config\", \"commit.gpgsign\", \"false\");\n680\t        RunGit(repoPath, \"config\", \"core.autocrlf\", \"false\");\n681\t        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(repoPath, \"README.md\"), \"# plan-guardrail-phase-test\");\n682\t        RunGit(repoPath, \"add\", \".\");\n683\t        RunGit(repoPath, \"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\");\n684\t    }\n685\t\n686\t    private static string RunGit(string workingDir, params string[] args)\n687\t    {\n688\t        var psi = new ProcessStartInfo(\"git\")\n689\t        {\n690\t            WorkingDirectory = workingDir,\n691\t            RedirectStandardOutput = true,\n692\t            RedirectStandardError = true,\n693\t            UseShellExecute = false\n694\t        };\n695\t        foreach (string arg in args) psi.ArgumentList.Add(arg);\n696\t        using var proc = Process.Start(psi)!;\n697\t        string stdout = proc.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();\n698\t        string stderr = proc.StandardError.ReadToEnd();\n699\t        proc.WaitForExit();\n700\t        if (proc.ExitCode != 0)\n701\t            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n702\t                $\"git {string.Join(\" \", args)} (in {workingDir}) exited {proc.ExitCode}: {stderr.Trim()}\");\n703\t        return stdout;\n704\t    }\n705\t\n706\t    /// <summary>Windows-safe recursive delete (strips the read-only bit git leaves on loose objects).</summary>\n707\t    private static void SafeDeleteTree(string root)\n708\t    {\n709\t the exit code.</summary>\n    private static async Task<int> RunCliAsync(params string[] args)\n    {\n        var io = new StringConsoleIo();\n        var root = CommandFactory.BuildRootCommand(io);\n        return await root.Parse(args).InvokeAsync();\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>As <see cref=\"RunCliAsync\"/> but also returns the captured console stdout A forced-fail spy\n    /// rolls exactly that non-FF sibling back to <c>needs-human</c> — an outcome only the DURING-RUN per-union\n    /// re-verify can produce (the end-of-run terminal gate fires only when the whole run is green). The spy\n    /// also captures that the re-verify carried the integration set, proving it was not absorbed.\n    /// GREEN on both current and new code by design.\n    /// </summary>\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task PerUnionIntegrationReVerify_StillFiresAtUnions_NotAbsorbedByTerminalFolder()\n    {\n        using var repo = new SiblingUnionRepo();\n\n        var spy = new SpyReVerifier { AlwaysPass = false };\n        var provider = new GitWorktreeProvider(repo.RepoPath, repo.WorktreeRoot);\n\n        RunReport report = await RunSchedulerAsync(repo.PlanDir, provider, spy);\n\n        // The per-union re-verify fired DURING the run...\n        Assert.True(spy.CallCount > 0,\n            \"the §4.3 per-union re-verify must still fire at a non-FF union — SpyReVerifier was never called\");\n\n        // ...carrying the scope:\"integration\" set (not an empty/absorbed set)...\n        Assert.Contains(spy.CallGuardrails, set => set.Any(g =>\n            string.Equals(g.Scope, \"integration\", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)));\n\n        // ...and forced-failed, rolling exactly the non-FF sibling back to needs-human (one sibling FF's green).\n        Assert.Single(report.Tasks, t => t.Outcome == TaskOutcome.NeedsHuman);\n        Assert.Single(report.Tasks, t => t.Outcome == TaskOutcome.Succeeded);\n    }\n\n    // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n    // Behaviour 5 (issue #240) — the terminal phase's success path is bracketed with plain console\n    // lines, matching Full Flight Checks' treatment (PlanPreflightPhaseTests). ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task TerminalGate_Green_PrintsRunningThenPassed_ToConsole()\n    {\n        using var plan = new PlanGuardrailPlan(worktree: false);\n        plan.HandFixTerminalGate(); // green from the start — this run should settle wholly green.\n\n        (int exit, string output) = await RunCliCapturedAsync(\"run\", plan.PlanDir, \"--no-ui\", \"--no-log-server\");\n\n        Assert.Equal(ExitCodes.Success, exit);\n        Assert.Contains(\"Terminal Gate: running...\", output);\n        Assert.Contains(\"Terminal Gate: passed.\", output);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task NoPlanGuardrails_PrintsNothingAboutTerminalGate()\n    {\n        // A plan with no <plan>/guardrails/ folder at all opts out of this phase entirely (SSOT §7) —\n        // printing \"Terminal Gate: running...\" for a plan that never declared one would be noise, not\n        // signal.\n        using var plan = new ScriptPlanBuilder().AddTask(\"01-a\");\n\n        (int exit, string output) = await RunCliCapturedAsync(\"run\", plan.PlanDir, \"--no-ui\", \"--no-log-server\");\n\n        Assert.Equal(ExitCodes.Success, exit);\n        Assert.DoesNotContain(\"Terminal Gate\", output);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task TerminalGate_Red_PrintsRunningButNotPassed()\n    {\n        // The default PlanGuardrailPlan fixture starts with a RED terminal check — the phase DOES\n        // run (the DAG settles green first), so \"running...\" must print, but it fails, so \"passed.\"\n        // must NOT — the existing PrintTerminalGateFailure path (asserted elsewhere) carries the\n        // failure detail instead.\n        using var plan = new PlanGuardrailPlan(worktree: false);\n\n        (int exit, string output) = await RunCliCapturedAsync(\"run\", plan.PlanDir, \"--no-ui\", \"--no-log-server\");\n\n        Assert.Equal(ExitCodes.TaskFailed, exit);\n        Assert.Contains(\"Terminal Gate: running...\", output);\n        Assert.DoesNotContain(\"Terminal Gate: passed.\", output);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Hand-wire the REAL <see cref=\"Scheduler\"/> over the given worktree provider + re-verifier, exactly as\n    /// MergeLockAndSettleTests / TopologyReuseForkSchedulerTests do. Load-only (no validate) so the fan-out\n    /// plan need not carry a terminal folder for this per-union pin.\n    /// </summary>\n    private static async Task<RunReport> RunSchedulerAsync(\n        string planDir, IWorktreeProvider provider, IReVerifier reVerifier)\n    {\n        PlanLoadResult load = new PlanLoader().Load(planDir);\n        Assert.NotNull(load.Plan);\n        Assert.False(load.HasErrors, string.Join(\"\\n\", load.Diagnostics));\n\n        var stateManager = new StateManager(load.Plan!.PlanDirectory);\n        stateManager.Initialize();\n\n        RunJournal journal = RunJournal.LoadOrCreate(load.Plan!);\n\n        var registry = PromptRunnerRegistry.Build(load.Plan!.Config,\n            _ => throw new InvalidOperationException(\"No prompt runners in the plan-guardrail per-union test.\"));\n        var interpreterMap = new InterpreterMap(new PathExecutableProbe(), load.Plan!.Config.Interpreters);\n\n        var executor = new TaskExecutor(\n            load.Plan!, new ProcessRunner(), interpreterMap, stateManager, journal, IRunObserver.Null, registry);\n\n        var scheduler = new Scheduler(\n            load.Plan!, executor, journal, worktreeProvider: provider, reVerifier: reVerifier);\n\n        return await scheduler.RunAsync(load.Plan!, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);\n    }\n\n    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    // SpyReVerifier — records every re-verify call and the guardrail set it carried.\n    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    private sealed class SpyReVerifier : IReVerifier\n    {\n        public int CallCount { get; private set; }\n        public bool AlwaysPass { get; init; } = true;\n        public List<IReadOnlyList<GuardrailDefinition>> CallGuardrails { get; } = [];\n\n        public Task<ReVerifyResult> ReVerifyAsync(\n            string worktreePath,\n            IReadOnlyList<GuardrailDefinition> guardrails,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            CallCount++;\n            CallGuardrails.Add(guardrails);\n            return Task.FromResult(new ReVerifyResult { Passed = AlwaysPass });\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    // PlanGuardrailPlan — a linear-chain plan (01-a → 02-b, both green) plus a plan-level\n    // <plan>/guardrails/ terminal gate. Serial (workspace \".\", maxParallelism 1, no git) or worktree\n    // (workspace \"..\", maxParallelism 2, inside a temp git repo at <repo>/plan).\n    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    private sealed class PlanGuardrailPlan : IDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly string _root;\n\n        public string PlanDir { get; }\n        public string? RepoPath { get; }\n\n        /// <summary>Absolute log every task action APPENDS its id to (outside any worktree) — proves no re-run.</summary>\n        public string ActionRanLogPath { get; }\n\n        /// <summary>The terminal <c>&lt;plan&gt;/guardrails/</c> check file, overwritten by the hand-fix.</summary>\n        public string TerminalCheckPath { get; }\n\n        /// <summary>The plan branch a worktree run integrates onto: <c>guardrails/&lt;plan-folder-name&gt;</c>.</summary>\n        public string PlanBranch => \"guardrails/\" + Path.GetFileName(PlanDir);\n\n        /// <summary>How many times a task action has run (append-log line count) — unchanged across a resume.</summary>\n        public int ActionRunCount => File.Exists(ActionRanLogPath)\n            ? File.ReadAllLines(ActionRanLogPath).Count(l => l.Trim().Length > 0)\n            : 0;\n\n        public PlanGuardrailPlan(bool worktree)\n        {\n            _root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), \"gr-plangr-\" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString(\"N\"));\n            Directory.CreateDirectory(_root);\n            ActionRanLogPath = Path.Combine(_root, \"action-ran.log\");\n\n            if (worktree)\n            {\n                RepoPath = Path.Combine(_root, \"repo\");\n                Directory.CreateDirectory(RepoPath);\n                InitRepo(RepoPath);\n                PlanDir = Path.Combine(RepoPath, \"plan\");\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                PlanDir = Path.Combine(_root, \"plan\");\n            }\n\n            Directory.CreateDirectory(PlanDir);\n            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(PlanDir, \"state\"));\n            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(PlanDir, \"tasks\"));\n\n            int maxParallelism = worktree ? 2 : 1;\n            string workspace = worktree ? \"..\" : \".\";\n            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(PlanDir, \"guardrails.json\"),\n                \"{\\n\" +\n                \"  \\\"version\\\": 1,\\n\" +\n                \"  \\\"guardrailMode\\\": \\\"failFast\\\",\\n\" +\n                \"  \\\"workspace\\\": \\\"\" + workspace + \"\\\",\\n\" +\n                \"  \\\"defaultRetries\\\": 0,\\n\" +\n                \"  \\\"maxParallelism\\\": \" + maxParallelism + \"\\n\" +\n                \"}\\n\");\n\n            // Linear chain: one leaf, no fan-in ⇒ the worktree content-teeth rule (GR2028) is exempt, so a\n            // plain exit-1 terminal check validates clean. Reject the combinations that would\n89\t            // otherwise silently no-op (or, for --fresh, destroy the very state being verified).\n90\t            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(revalidateTask))\n91\t            {\n92\t                if (fresh || dryRun)\n93\t                {\n94\t                    io.Out.WriteLine(\"--revalidate-task cannot be combined with --fresh or --dry-run.\");\n95\t                    return ExitCodes.HarnessError;\n96\t                }\n97\t\n98\t                return await Revalidate.ExecuteAsync(folder, revalidateTask, io, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n99\t            }\n100\t\n101\t            if (dryRun)\n102\t            {\n103\t                return DryRun.Execute(folder, io, skipReviewCheck);\n104\t            }\n105\t\n106\t            return await RunAsync(folder, fresh, noUi, noLogServer, logPort, mergeOnSuccess, skipReviewCheck, io, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n107\t        });\n108\t\n109\t        return command;\n110\t    }\n111\t\n112\t    private static async Task<int> RunAsync(\n113\t        string folder, bool fresh, bool noUi, bool noLogServer, int logPort, bool mergeOnSuccess, bool skipReviewCheck, IConsoleIo io, CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n114\t    {\n115\t        PlanProbe.Result probe = PlanProbe.LoadAndValidate(folder);\n116\t        if (probe.HasErrors || probe.Plan is null)\n117\t        {\n118\t            PlanProbe.PrintDiagnostics(probe.Diagnostics, io.Out);\n119\t            io.Out.WriteLine(\"\\nValidation failed; nothing was run.\");\n120\t            return ExitCodes.HarnessError;\n121\t        }\n122\t\n123\t        // Review-marker nudge (warn, never block — SSOT §13, issue #79): if the plan hasn't been\n124\t        // through /guardrails-review (or has changed since), print a one-line warning before running,\n125\t        // unless --skip-review-check. Reuses the same deterministic evaluation as `validate`.\n126\t        WarnIfUnreviewed(probe.Plan, skipReviewCheck, io);\n127\t\n128\t        // --merge-on-success forces end-of-run delivery on (SSOT §5.3 / §2: \"CLI --merge-on-success\n129\t        // overrides\"). The flag only augments — it can turn the config value on, never off — so a\n130\t        // plan that already set mergeOnSuccess: true is unaffected.\n131\t        if (mergeOnSuccess && !probe.Plan.Config.MergeOnSuccess)\n132\t        {\n133\t            probe = probe with { Plan = probe.Plan with { Config = probe.Plan.Config with { MergeOnSuccess = true } } };\n134\t        }\n135\t\n136\t        if (fresh)\n137\t        {\n138\t            RunReset.Fresh(probe.Plan.PlanDirectory);\n139\t            io.Out.WriteLine(\"Fresh run: runtime state cleared and re-seeded.\\n\");\n140\t        }\n141\t\n142\t        bool live = !noUi && AnsiConsole.Profile.Capabilities.Interactive && !Console.IsOutputRedirected;\n143\t\n144\t        // Resolve the run's id up-front so the live log server and the post-mortem links target the\n145\t        // correct logs/<runId>/ tree (SSOT §8/§12). LoadOrCreate is idempotent: it creates run.json\n146\t        // here (or reads it on resume), and the Scheduler's own LoadOrCreate then reads the SAME\n147\t        // run.json — so this runId matches the one the executor writes attempt logs under.\n148\t        RunJournal journal = RunJournal.LoadOrCreate(probe.Plan);\n149\t        string runId = journal.Document.RunId;\n150\t\n151\t        // Pre-DAG plan-preflight phase (SSOT §7, deliverable 3): evaluate <plan>/preflights/ ONCE,\n152\t        // BEFORE the Scheduler builds any wave, against the run's starting bytes. A red preflight halts\n153\t        // HERE — no task runs, zero tokens spent — journaled as planPreflights.status =\n154\t        // plan-preflight-failed (a top-level section OUTSIDE tasks{}). A passed marker whose planHash\n155\t        // still matches the current plan is SKIPPED on resume rather than re-evaluated (the B1 fix).\n156\t        // Issue #240: this phase (and the terminal one below) previously ran entirely silently on\n157\t        // success — no row in the live table (its lifetime doesn't even span these phases, #240's own\n158\t        // investigation), no console line at all. Bracket with plain WriteLines, guarded on the plan\n159\t        // actually declaring this folder (EvaluateAsync itself no-ops for free when it doesn't, so\n160\t        // printing \"running...\" then would be misleading noise for a plan that never opted in).\n161\t        bool hasPlanPreflights = probe.Plan.PlanPreflights.Count > 0;\n162\t        if (hasPlanPreflights)\n163\t        {\n164\t            io.Out.WriteLine(\"Full Flight Checks: running...\");\n165\t        }\n166\t\n167\t        bool preflightsPassed = await PlanPreflightPhase\n168\t            .EvaluateAsync(probe.Plan, journal, new ProcessRunner(), cancellationToken)\n169\t            .ConfigureAwait(false);\n170\t\n171\t        if (hasPlanPreflights && preflightsPassed)\n172\t        {\n173\t            io.Out.WriteLine(\"Full Flight Checks: passed.\");\n174\t        }\n175\t\n176\t        if (!preflightsPassed)\n177\t        {\n178\t            io.Out.WriteLine();\n179\t            io.Out.WriteLine(\"Plan preflight FAILED — halting before scheduling any task (SSOT §7 planPreflights).\");\n180\t            io.Out.WriteLine($\"  See {RunJournal.PathFor(probe.Plan.PlanDirectory)} (\\\"planPreflights\\\") for the failed check(s).\");\n181\t            return ExitCodes.TaskFailed;\n182\t        }\n183\t\n184\t        // The log server is a companion to the live table: start it only in the interactive path\n185\t        // (nobody clicks links in CI / redirected output), and never let a binding failure abort\n186\t        // the run — TryStart returns null and prints one warning.\n187\t        LogServer? logServer = (live && !noLogServer)\n188\t            ? LogServer.TryStart(probe.Plan.PlanDirectory, runId, probe.Plan.Tasks, logPort, io.Out)\n189\t            : null;\n190\t\n191\t        try\n192\t        {\n193\t            if (logServer is not null)\n194\t            {\n195\t                // The canonical \"all tasks\" page is the static index file (printed by\n196\t                // PrintStaticIndexLink below); this http server is just the tailing backend that the\n197\t                // static index links a RUNNING task to (issue #143). De-emphasised accordingly.\n198\t                io.Out.WriteLine($\"Live tailing server (active tasks): {logServer.BaseUrl}\\n\");\n199\t            }\n200\t\n201\t            Func<string, string?>? logUrlForTask = logServer is null ? null : logServer.UrlForTask;\n202\t\n203\t            // The on-the-fly static site (issue #141 item 2) is written for BOTH the live and the\n204\t            // --no-ui paths — a file:// \"all tasks\" page that updates as tasks settle, useful headless\n205\t            // or interactive. It lives under logs/<runId>/, the same tree the executor writes attempts\n206\t            // into and the live server serves. The inner observer (live table or plain console) is\n207\t            // wrapped so the site is rewritten after each forwarded event.\n208\t            string logsRoot = Path.Combine(probe.Plan.PlanDirectory, \"logs\", runId);\n209\t\n210\t            RunReport report;\n211\t            if (live)\n212\t            {\n213\t                // Write the initial all-pending index AND print its link BEFORE constructing\n214\t                // LiveRunObserver — its ctor starts the Spectre AnsiConsole.Live region, and any\n215\t                // console write into an active Live region corrupts the table (#145 Bug 1). So the\n216\t                // static-index write + its link must precede the live region.\n217\t                OnTheFlyLogSiteObserver.WriteInitialIndex(logsRoot, runId, probe.Plan.Tasks, logUrlForTask);\n218\t                PrintStaticIndexLink(logsRoot, io);    // \"all tasks\" page link at run START\n219\t\n220\t                await using var liveObserver = new LiveRunObserver(probe.Plan.Tasks, logUrlForTask, probe.Plan.PlanDirectory, runId);\n221\t                var siteObserver = new OnTheFlyLogSiteObserver(liveObserver, logsRoot, runId, probe.Plan.Tasks, logUrlForTask);\n222\t                report = await ExecuteAsync(probe.Plan, siteObserver, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n223\t            }\n224\t            else\n225\t            {\n226\t                var siteObserver = new OnTheFlyLogSiteObserver(\n227\t                    new ConsoleRunObserver(io.Out), logsRoot, runId, probe.Plan.Tasks, logUrlForTask);\n228\t                siteObserver.WriteInitialIndex();\n229\t                PrintStaticIndexLink(logsRoot, io);\n230\t                report = await ExecuteAsync(probe.Plan, siteObserver, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n231\t            }\n232\t\n233\t            // Terminal plan-guardrail phase (SSOT §7/§7.1, deliverable 4): evaluate <plan>/guardrails/\n234\t            // ONCE, AFTER the DAG drains wholly green, against the merged plan-branch HEAD — replacing\n235\t            // the retired integrationGate task-kind's terminal role (Scheduler.cs skips that legacy path\n236\t            // whenever the plan declares this folder, SSOT §3.3). No-op (true) when the DAG did not\n237\t            // fully succeed this run/resume, or the plan has no <plan>/guardrails/ folder at all.\n238\t            // B2(b) terminal-only resume falls out for free: a resume where every task is already\n239\t            // succeeded drains the DAG with nothing left to do (report.AllSucceeded stays true, no\n240\t            // attempt burned), so this phase unconditionally re-fires against the current HEAD.\n241\t            // Issue #240: same silent-on-success gap as Full Flight Checks above. This phase is only\n242\t            // ever actually invoked when the DAG settled green AND the plan declares this folder (the\n243\t            // `!report.AllSucceeded ||` short-circuit means EvaluateAsync is never called at all\n244\t            // otherwise) — gate the bracketing lines on exactly that, or \"Terminal Gate: running...\"\n245\t            // would misleadingly print for a run that failed before ever reaching this phase.\n246\t            bool hasPlanGuardrails = probe.Plan.PlanGuardrails.Count > 0;\n247\t            bool willEvaluateTerminalGate = report.AllSucceeded && hasPlanGuardrails;\n248\t            if (willEvaluateTerminalGate)\n249\t            {\n250\t                io.Out.WriteLine(\"Terminal Gate: running...\");\n251\t            }\n252\t\n253\t            bool planGuardrailsPassed = !report.AllSucceeded\n254\t                || await PlanGuardrailPhase.EvaluateAsync(probe.Plan, new ProcessRunner(), cancellationToken)\n255\t                    .ConfigureAwait(false);\n256\t\n257\t            if (willEvaluateTerminalGate && planGuardrailsPassed)\n258\t            {\n259\t                io.Out.WriteLine(\"Terminal Gate: passed.\");\n260\t            }\n261\t\n262\t            int exitCode = Finish(report, probe.Plan, runId, io);\n263\t            if (report.AllSucceeded && !planGuardrailsPassed)\n264\t            {\n265\t                PrintTerminalGateFailure(probe.Plan.PlanDirectory, io);\n266\t                return ExitCodes.TaskFailed;\n267\t            }\n268\t\n269\t            return exitCode;\n270\t        }\n271\t        finally\n272\t        {\n273\t            if (logServer is not null)\n274\t            {\n275\t                await logServer.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);\n276\t            }\n277\t        }\n278\t    }\n279\t\n280\t    /// <summary>\n281\t    /// Print the review-marker nudge (GR2025, WARNING — SSOT §13, issue #79) when the plan is\n282\t    /// missing/stale a <c>/guardrails-review</c> marker, unless <paramref name=\"skipReviewCheck\"/>.\n283\t    /// Warn, never block — the run proceeds regardless. Shared by <c>run</c> and <c>--dry-run</c>.\n284\t    /// </summary>\n285\t    public static void WarnIfUnreviewed(Core.Model.PlanDefinition plan, bool skipReviewCheck, IConsoleIo io)\n286\t    {\n287\t        if (skipReviewCheck)\n288\t        {\n289\t            return;\n290\t        }\n291\t\n292\t        if (Core.Loading.PlanValidator.ReviewMarkerDiagnostic(plan) is { } nudge)\n293\t        {\n294\t            io.Out.WriteLine(nudge.ToString());\n295\t            io.Out.WriteLine();\n296\t        }\n297\t    }\n298\t\n299\t    /// <summary>Print the summary and map the report to the process exit code (SSOT §7).</summary>\n300\t    private static int Finish(RunReport report, Core.Model.PlanDefinition plan, string runId, IConsoleIo io)\n301\t    {\n302\t        string planDirectory = plan.PlanDirectory;\n303\t        string logsRoot = Path.Combine(planDirectory, \"logs\", runId);\n304\t\n305\t        // Write the DURABLE final site (issue #141 item 2): all-static links, NO meta-refresh, every\n306\t        // task page — so the artifact left on disk is complete and self-contained (identical to\n307\t        // `logs --export`). The during-run writer left a refreshing index with live links; this\n308\t        // replaces it. Best-effort: a render hiccup must never change the run's exit code.\n309\t        WriteDurableFinalSite(logsRoot, plan, planDirectory);\n310\t\n311\t        PrintSummary(report, planDirectory, runId, io);\n312\t\n313\t        // The \"all tasks\" static page link at run END (alongside the post-mortem logs pointer).\n314\t        PrintStaticIndexLink(logsRoot, io);\n315\t\n316\t        // Issue #150 — honest halt for an infrastructure fault. The scheduler returned an ABORTED\n317\t        // report instead of throwing; render a one-line diagnostic + remedy as the headline, write\n318\t        // the FULL exception to the run logs (a dev tool keeps the detail, just not as the headline),\n319\t        // and exit non-zero — never a raw unhandled stack trace as the headline.\n320\t        if (report.Abort is { } abort)\n321\t        {\n322\t            WriteAbortDetailToLogs(logsRoot, abort);\n323\t            io.Out.WriteLine();\n324\t            io.Out.WriteLine($\"RUN ABORTED: {abort.Headline}\");\n325\t            io.Out.WriteLine($\"  {abort.Remedy}\");\n326\t            io.Out.WriteLine($\"  Full fault detail written to {Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(logsRoot, \"abort.log\"))}\");\n327\t            return ExitCodes.HarnessError;\n328\t        }\n329\t\n330\t        if (report.Cancelled)\n331\t        {\n332\t            return ExitCodes.Cancelled;\n333\t        }\n334\t\n335\t        // Issue #150 — a wholly-green run whose end-of-run delivery to the user's branch was HALTED\n336\t        // (a git hook rejected the user-facing merge, a conflict, or a dirty user tree) is NOT a\n337\t        // clean success: the work is durable on the plan branch, but the user must act. Render the\n338\t        // actionable message and exit non-zero. A FastForwarded/Merged delivery, or no mergeOnSuccess\n339\t        // at all (null), leaves the success verdict untouched.\n340\t        if (report.AllSucceeded\n341\t            && report.MergeOnSuccessOutcome is { } mergeOutcome\n342\t            && mergeOutcome is not (MergeOnSuccessResult.FastForwarded or MergeOnSuccessResult.Merged))\n343\t        {\n344\t            PrintMergeOnSuccessHalt(report, plan, mergeOutcome, io);\n345\t            return ExitCodes.TaskFailed;\n346\t        }\n347\t\n348\t        return report.AllSucceeded ? ExitCodes.Success : ExitCodes.TaskFailed;\n349\t    }\n350\t\n351\t    /// <summary>\n352\t    /// Print the terminal plan-guardrail gate failure (D4). Read the failed checks (name + reason) that\n353\t    /// <see cref=\"PlanGuardrailPhase\"/> journaled into <c>planGuardrails.failedChecks</c> and surface each\n354\t    /// one INLINE — so a terminal halt is as legible as the legacy per-task gate (which listed its failed\n355\t    /// guardrails in the summary), instead of a bare \"see planGuardrails in run.json\" pointer that forces\n356\t    /// the user to open the journal. Mirrors the shape of the NEEDS HUMAN block. Best-effort: a journal\n357\t    /// read hiccup falls back to the generic pointer rather than throwing (the exit code is unaffected).\n358\t    /// <para>\n359\t    /// It ALSO surfaces the #175 merge-collision hint (SSOT §3.3, issue #205) that\n360\t    /// <see cref=\"PlanGuardrailPhase\"/> journals into <c>planGuardrails.collisionHint</c> when ≥2 tasks\n361\t    /// have overlapping <c>writeScope</c> on a shared file — the same attribution the legacy per-task gate\n362\t    /// carried in its summary, ported onto the terminal phase.\n363\t    /// </para>\n364\t    /// </summary>\n365\t    private static void PrintTerminalGateFailure(string planDirectory, IConsoleIo io)\n366\t    {\n367\t        TextWriter output = io.Out;\n368\t        string journalPath = RunJournal.PathFor(planDirectory);\n369\t\n370\t        output.WriteLine();\n371\t        output.WriteLine(\"Plan guardrail gate FAILED on the merged HEAD — terminal halt (SSOT §7 planGuardrails).\");\n372\t\n373\t        PlanGuardrailsSection? section = TryReadPlanGuardrailSection(journalPath);\n374\t        IReadOnlyList<FailedGuardrail> failedChecks = section?.FailedChecks ?? [];\n375\t        if (failedChecks.Count > 0)\n376\t        {\n377\t            foreach (FailedGuardrail check in failedChecks)\n378\t            {\n379\t                output.WriteLine($\"  FAILED: {check.Name} — {check.Reason}\");\n380\t            }\n381\t            output.WriteLine($\"  (full detail in {journalPath} under \\\"planGuardrails\\\")\");\n382\t        }\n383\t        else\n384\t        {\n385\t            // No structured checks readable (older/absent section, or a read hiccup): the prior pointer.\n386\t            output.WriteLine($\"  See {journalPath} (\\\"planGuardrails\\\") for the failed check(s).\");\n387\t        }\n388\t\n389\t        // #175/#205 merge-collision attribution — advisory, only present when writeScopes overlap.\n390\t        if (section?.CollisionHint is { Length: > 0 } collisionHint)\n391\t        {\n392\t            output.WriteLine($\"  {collisionHint}\");\n393\t        }\n394\t    }\n395\t\n396\t    /// <summary>\n397\t    /// Read the terminal gate's <c>planGuardrails</c> section (failed checks + the #175 collision hint)\n398\t    /// from the persisted journal. Returns null when the section is absent/passed or the journal cannot be\n399\t    /// read — the caller then falls back to the generic pointer.\n400\t    /// </summary>\n401\t    private static PlanGuardrailsSection? TryReadPlanGuardrailSection(string journalPath)\n402\t    {\n403\t        try\n404\t        {\n405\t            if (!File.Exists(journalPath))\n406\t            {\n407\t                return null;\n408\t            }\n409\t\n410\t            JournalDocument document = JournalReader.Read(journalPath);\n411\t            return document.PlanGuardrails;\n412\t        }\n413\t        catch (IOException)\n414\t        {\n415\t            return null;\n416\t        }\n417\t        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)\n418\t        {\n419\t            return null;\n420\t        }\n421\t        catch (JsonException)\n422\t        {\n423\t            return null;\n424\t        }\n425\t    }\n426\t\n427\t    /// <summary>\n428\t    /// Render the actionable end-of-run delivery halt (issue #150). The plan branch carries all the\n429\t    /// (verified) work; only the optional merge back into the user's branch was refused. For a hook\n430\t    /// rejection the user's own hook stderr (<see cref=\"RunReport.MergeOnSuccessDetail\"/>) is shown\n431\t    /// verbatim so they see exactly why and can resolve it or disable the hook for the merge.\n432\t    /// </summary>\n433\t    private static void PrintMergeOnSuccessHalt(\n434\t        RunReport report, Core.Model.PlanDefinition plan, MergeOnSuccessResult outcome, IConsoleIo io)\n435\t    {\n436\t        TextWriter output = io.Out;\n437\t        string planBranch = $\"guardrails/{Path.GetFileName(plan.PlanDirectory)}\";\n438\t\n439\t        output.WriteLine();\n440\t        switch (outcome)\n441\t        {\n442\t            case MergeOnSuccessResult.HookRejected:\n443\t                output.WriteLine(\n444\t                    $\"All tasks passed and are on branch `{planBranch}`. The final merge into your \" +\n445\t                    \"branch was rejected by your git hook:\");\n446\t                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(report.MergeOnSuccessDetail))\n447\t                {\n448\t                    output.WriteLine($\"  {report.MergeOnSuccessDetail}\");\n449\t                }\n450\t                output.WriteLine(\n451\t                    \"  Resolve and merge manually, or disable the hook for the merge. Your branch is \" +\n452\t                    \"unchanged (the merge was aborted).\");\n453\t                break;\n454\t\n455\t            case MergeOnSuccessResult.Conflict:\n456\t                output.WriteLine(\n457\t                    $\"All tasks passed and are on branch `{planBranch}`. The final merge into your \" +\n458\t                    \"branch CONFLICTED with a change made on your branch during the run; AI-merge is \" +\n459\t                    \"withheld here (SSOT §5.3). Your branch is unchanged — merge `\" + planBranch +\n460\t                    \"` manually.\");\n461\t                break;\n462\t\n463\t            case MergeOnSuccessResult.DirtyWorkingTree:\n464\t                output.WriteLine(\n465\t                    $\"All tasks passed and are on branch `{planBranch}`. The final merge into your \" +\n466\t                    \"branch was refused because your working tree has uncommitted changes. Commit or \" +\n467\t                    \"stash them, then merge `\" + planBranch + \"` manually.\");\n468\t                break;\n469\t        }\n470\t    }\n471\t\n472\t    /// <summary>\n473\t    /// Write an aborted run's FULL fault detail (issue #150) to <c>logs/&lt;runId&gt;/abort.log</c> so\n474\t    /// the console headline stays a one-liner while the dev keeps the whole exception. Best-effort —\n475\t    /// a logs-tree write hiccup must never change the run's exit code or mask the abort.\n476\t    /// </summary>\n477\t    private static void WriteAbortDetailToLogs(string logsRoot, Core.Execution.RunAbort abort)\n478\t    {\n479\t        try\n480\t        {\n481\t            Directory.CreateDirectory(logsRoot);\n482\t            File.WriteAllText(\n483\t                Path.Combine(logsRoot, \"abort.log\"),\n484\t                $\"{abort.Headline}\\n\\n{abort.Remedy}\\n\\n--- full fault detail ---\\n{abort.Detail}\\n\");\n485\t        }\n486\t        catch (IOException) { /* best-effort */ }\n487\t        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException) { /* best-effort */ }\n488\t    }\n489\t\n490\t    /// <summary>\n491\t    /// Render the durable, self-contained static site (all-static links, no refresh, every task page)\n492\t    /// at run end via <see cref=\"LogSiteRenderer.ExportSite\"/>, reading the freshly-persisted journal\n493\t    /// for per-task status. No-op (and never throws) when the journal is absent — a fully-resumed /\n494\t    /// all-skipped run writes no logs, so there is nothing to render.\n495\t    /// </summary>\n496\t    private static void WriteDurableFinalSite(string logsRoot, Core.Model.PlanDefinition plan, string planDirectory)\n497\t    {\n498\t        string journalPath = RunJournal.PathFor(planDirectory);\n499\t        if (!File.Exists(journalPath) || !Directory.Exists(logsRoot))\n500\t        {\n501\t            return;\n502\t        }\n503\t\n504\t        try\n505\t        {\n506\t            JournalDocument document = JournalReader.Read(journalPath);\n507\t            LogSiteRenderer.ExportSite(logsRoot, plan.Tasks, document);\n508\t        }\n509\t        catch (IOException)\n510\t        {\n511\t            // Best-effort durable site — a transient lock must never flip the run's exit code.\n512\t        }\n513\t        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)\n514\t        {\n515\t            // ditto — a logs-tree permission hiccup is not a run failure.\n516\t        }\n517\t    }\n518\t\n519\t    /// <summary>\n520\t    /// Print a clickable <c>file://</c> link to the run's static \"all tasks\" index\n521\t    /// (<c>logs/&lt;runId&gt;/index.html</c>, issue #141 item 2) — the during-run refreshing page at run\n522\t    /// start, the durable page at run end. Emits an OSC 8 hyperlink only when the terminal can render\n523\t    /// one (matching the post-mortem pointer's gate); otherwise the plain absolute path, which is\n524\t    /// copy-pasteable. No-op when the index does not exist (nothing was rendered).\n525\t    /// </summary>\n526\t    private static void PrintStaticIndexLink(string logsRoot, IConsoleIo io)\n527\t    {\n528\t        string indexPath = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(logsRoot, \"index.html\"));\n529\t        if (!File.Exists(indexPath))\n530\t        {\n531\t            return;\n532\t        }\n533\t\n534\t        bool linkable = !Console.IsOutputRedirected && AnsiConsole.Profile.Capabilities.Links;\n535\t        io.Out.WriteLine($\"All tasks (static log site): {Hyperlink(indexPath, linkable)}\");\n536\t    }\n537\t\n538\t    private static Task<RunReport> ExecuteAsync(\n539\t        Core.Model.PlanDefinition plan,\n540\t        IRunObserver observer,\n541\t        CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n542\t    {\n543\t        Scheduler scheduler = SchedulerFactory.Create(plan, new ProcessRunner(), new PathExecutableProbe(), observer);\n544\t        return scheduler.RunAsync(plan, cancellationToken);\n545\t    }\n546\t\n547\t    private static void PrintSummary(RunReport report, string planDirectory, string runId, IConsoleIo io)\n548\t    {\n549\t        TextWriter output = io.Out;\n550\t\n551\t        output.WriteLine(\"Summary\");\n552\t        output.WriteLine(\"-------\");\n553\t        foreach (TaskResult result in report.Tasks)\n554\t        {\n555\t            output.WriteLine($\"  {StatusLabel(result.Outcome),-16} {result.TaskId,-32} {result.Summary}\");\n556\t        }\n557\t\n558\t        int green = report.Tasks.Count(t => t.IsGreen);\n559\t        output.WriteLine();\n560\t        output.WriteLine(report.Cancelled\n561\t            ? $\"Run CANCELLED — {green}/{report.Tasks.Count} task(s) green; in-flight tasks journaled pending. Re-run to resume.\"\n562\t            : $\"{green}/{report.Tasks.Count} task(s) green (succeeded or skipped).\");\n563\t\n564\t        PrintTotalCost(planDirectory, output);\n565\t\n566\t        // Post-mortem pointer for EVERY task, not just failures: a green task whose guardrails\n567\t        // turned out too weak is reviewed from the same on-disk logs (action output, guardrail\n568\t        // stdout, feedback per attempt). The link target is the ABSOLUTE logs/<runId>/ root so it is\n569\t        // clickable (issue #59); the <task-id>/attempt-N/ layout follows as guidance text. The\n570\t        // per-attempt artifacts live under logs/<runId>/ (SSOT §8), NOT the pre-plan-08 state/logs/.\n571\t        string logsRoot = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(planDirectory, \"logs\", runId));\n572\t        string sep = Path.DirectorySeparatorChar.ToString();\n573\t        // Emit a clickable OSC 8 link only when the terminal can actually render one — matching the\n574\t        // live table's gate. Redirection alone is too weak: a non-redirected but hyperlink-incapable\n575\t        // TTY would get raw escape bytes as visible garbage, which Spectre's link capability check\n576\t        // avoids. Also require the target to exist — a full-resume/all-skipped run writes no logs, so\n577\t        // don't advertise a link that 404s. When not linkable the plain absolute path still serves as\n578\t        // copy-pasteable guidance and fixes the #59 regression (it was relative with literal placeholders).\n579\t        bool linkable = !Console.IsOutputRedirected\n580\t                        && AnsiConsole.Profile.Capabilities.Links\n581\t                        && Directory.Exists(logsRoot);\n582\t        output.WriteLine();\n583\t        output.WriteLine($\"Logs (post-mortem any task — pass or fail): {Hyperlink(logsRoot, linkable)}\");\n584\t        output.WriteLine($\"  each task's attempts are under <task-id>{sep}attempt-N{sep}\");\n585\t\n586\t        PrintNeedsHumanSections(report, logsRoot, output);\n587\t    }\n588\t\n589\t    /// <summary>\n590\t    /// Print the post-run NEEDS HUMAN sections, resolving each task's triage diagnosis from the\n591\t    /// on-disk <c>triage.json</c> sidecar in its task-level log dir. Thin production wrapper over the\n592\t    /// pure <see cref=\"RenderNeedsHumanSections\"/> (which is unit-tested with an injected resolver).\n593\t    /// </summary>\n594\t    private static void PrintNeedsHumanSections(RunReport report, string logsRoot, TextWriter output) =>\n595\t        RenderNeedsHumanSections(\n596\t            report.Tasks, logsRoot, output,\n597\t            taskLogDir => TriageSummaryReader.TryRead(taskLogDir));\n598\t\n599\t    /// <summary>\n600\t    /// Render the post-run NEEDS HUMAN sections (issue #163): per failed/needs-human task, surface the\n601\t    /// AI triage root-cause CATEGORY + one-line diagnosis (and the drafted GH-issue title when present)\n602\t    /// directly in the console — so the user does not open each <c>feedback.md</c>. When several tasks\n603\t    /// share a diagnosis category the repeat is annotated (\"same root cause as …\") so one fix resolving\n604\t    /// several failures is obvious at a glance. A task with no structured triage (unstructured or failed\n605\t    /// — <paramref name=\"triageFor\"/> returns null) renders the prior shape, unchanged. The leading line\n606\t    /// stays parseable: <c>NEEDS HUMAN: &lt;task-id&gt; — &lt;summary&gt;</c>.\n607\t    /// <para>\n608\t    /// Pure (no IO) — the triage lookup is injected as <paramref name=\"triageFor\"/> (the task-level log\n609\t    /// dir → <see cref=\"TriageSummary\"/>), so the production path reads the sidecar and tests inject a\n610\t    /// fake.
